Security runway slice (2026-07-26, `#1345`):
- **The inherited HIGH SQLite native advisory is remediated without bypassing Central Package Management.** `Taskdeck.Infrastructure` directly references the centrally pinned `SQLitePCLRaw.bundle_e_sqlite3` 3.0.5, so bundle, config, core, provider, and native components resolve as one 3.0.5 family. The loaded SQLite runtime is 3.53.4, above the advisory's 3.50.2 security floor; a host-native integration test opens an in-memory database and enforces that floor. The 3.0.5 refresh passed restore, vulnerability audit, Release build, native runtime verification, and exact-head hosted CI before merge `d1ac680b`. The original security-floor delivery also proved the EF model and a self-contained `win-x64` `/health/ready` smoke at 2.1.12; those two platform-specific checks were not repeated specifically for 3.0.5.

Project-automation runway slice (2026-07-26, `#1458`):
- **The ProjectV2 priority helper now establishes completeness instead of trusting a capped sample.** It follows cursor pages to a stable item-count/project-stamp snapshot, uses ordinal identity for GitHub node IDs and cursors, rejects truncated nested connections, treats a positive `-Limit` as a fail-closed ceiling, and derives PR priority from complete repository-aware closing links/body references. Before `-Apply`, it validates all option mappings and rebuilds the complete source-derived plan; any drift aborts before the first write, and a complete post-apply audit remains mandatory. Its authentication-free self-test covers the >1,000-item and failure boundaries.
- **The live project-priority cleanup is complete.** On 2026-07-27, the 130 previously unlabeled Taskdeck Issues were classified and verified 130/130 with exactly one Priority label (`I=5`, `II=9`, `III=2`, `IV=18`, `V=96`). External Issue references are visible non-authority: their labels never rank, external-only PRs derive `Priority V`, and an external closing Issue still permits same-repository body fallback. After the closing link reduced the plan to 141 real field drifts, the reviewed Apply succeeded 141/141; its built-in audit, two fresh pre-merge audits, and the final post-merge handoff audit each proved 1,472/1,472 items, 12 ignored external references, and zero remaining drift. PR #1488 merged as `84674979`, and issue #1458 plus both project items are `Done / Priority I`.

PR-queue clearing wave (2026-07-25, **8 PRs merged**, and the open-PR queue is down to one human-gated draft):
- **`#1469` (`#1462`) — the frontend `Proposal` type exposes `approvedRevisionId`,** declared **required** rather than optional because `MapToDto` always assigns it and the API serializes with default options, so an unpinned proposal arrives as an explicit `null` and never an absent key — which also turns a field-by-field rebuild that drops the pin into a compile error. Contract exposure only; no UI claim, per `#1298`. Held against a dead-code sweep by an exported type alias, because `tsconfig.app.json` excludes `src/tests/**` from typecheck (`#1468`, measured at **415** pre-existing errors when the exclusion is lifted).
- **`#1337` (`#1299`) established the original licensing pack; ADR-0050 supersedes its MIT-forever term.** On 2026-08-12 the root licence and current metadata moved to GPL-3.0-only while prior MIT grants were preserved in `LICENSES/MIT.txt`. `LICENSING.md` continues to state the never-gated free boundary and additive-only posture for any future commercial module; `ee/` remains reserved with a separately licensed placeholder. Its DCO guidance and advisory CI job were active at delivery, then paused by explicit maintainer decision on 2026-08-23; `#2019` tracks any future restoration. Name/trademark availability was researched and recorded on `#1299` (**no registered TASKDECK mark found in any register that could be queried** — USPTO, TMview and WIPO, each null result control-validated against a known-hit term; UKIPO's own register hard-blocked automated access, so GB coverage is indirect via TMview, which EUIPO states is not an official register. The real risk is **registrability**, not collision). Pre-commercial legal residuals — attorney registrability/TASKADE opinion, the UKIPO/EUIPO coverage gaps, perishable namespace reservations, and the keep-or-rename decision before `v0.1.0` — are tracked as **`#1482`**.
- **`#1477` + `#1481` (`#1123`) — the release pipeline is rehearsable without pushing a tag.** `create-release` had no condition and a bare `workflow_dispatch` resolved the version to the literal `refs/heads/main`, whose slashes broke packaging — so the pipeline could not be exercised at all before the one irreversible action `#1303` reserves to the maintainer. A blank-tag dispatch from a **branch** is now an explicit rehearsal (`v0.0.0-dryrun+<sha>`, builds, smoke-tests, uploads artifacts, publishes nothing); a tag push, a dispatch naming a tag, **or a dispatch whose ref is itself a tag** publishes. Rehearsed for real: all four platforms green, `create-release` skipped, **0 tags and 0 releases created**. Note the smoke test covers the **three runner-native RIDs** only — `osx-x64` is cross-arch on the arm64 macOS runner and ships with less release evidence than the other three.
- **`#1478` (`#1271`) — dogfooding is instrumented.** A dedicated `TASKDECK_DOGFOOD_DB` so dev/demo/E2E traffic cannot contaminate the signal, a read-only snapshot script (counts and dates only; safe to paste into an issue), a friction log, and a checkpoint rubric fixed before any real usage data existed. **The measured pre-dogfooding baseline is the finding:** 8 active days, none in the last 28, last activity 2026-04-23, 10 of 13 boards demo/test residue — a **2026-07-25 historical baseline**, superseded as a status claim by the dogfooding sprint that **started 2026-08-22** (walkthrough q-2/q-8; `docs/dogfooding/LOG.md`). Even then the loop itself worked: **17 of 20 proposals reached Apply**, counted by `AppliedAt`. A status-based count reads 1/20, because `Dismiss()` accepts an already-`Applied` proposal and overwrites `Status` — so filing away a finished item erases the evidence it applied. Seven adversarial review rounds; residual activity-source accuracy questions consolidated on **`#1480`**.
- **`#1471`, `#1472`, `#1479` — canonical-doc and human-action hygiene:** the `#1462` residual list reconciled across both canonical docs (and a wrong measurement of my own corrected, 383 → **415**); `#1457`'s `/hooks` trust gate and the recurring merged-worktree prune recorded as human-only items, which no file previously tracked; `#1273`/`#1235` checked off on explicit maintainer instruction (a recorded rule-3 exception).
- **`#1295` CLOSED as superseded, not merged.** The deny-floor overlay failed **13 of 13 of its own bypass tests** and targeted global floor v1.3.0 against an installed 1.6.5. Its 11 uncovered rule classes and 16 review threads remain historical producer evidence, but the 2026-07-31 maintainer decision retired the Taskdeck-local floor instead of rebuilding the overlay; generic gaps belong upstream in agent-harness.
- **Repository hardening:** `main` previously permitted **force-pushes and deletions**; both are now blocked (deliberately relaxed otherwise — no required checks, no required approvals). The old project-priority helper silently truncated at 1,000 items; a one-off 1,444-item snapshot repaired 288 mismatched fields (`#1474`), but that was not durable proof of a clean project. The complete, fail-closed contract and the remaining label/data gate are tracked by `#1458`.
- **Backlog triage (`#1270`):** 12 already-decided issues closed with dated pivot notes, after an adversarial pass **bounced 13 of 25 proposed closures** — including `#1123`, the v0.1 ship-gate item. `#1270` itself stays open: two of its own acceptance criteria are obsolete against ADR-0044 and REVIVAL_PLAN §6 and must not be executed as written.
- **Seeded this wave:** `#1470`, `#1473`, `#1474`, `#1475` (the repo hook denied 6 of 8 *benign* commands), `#1476` (the then-configured local smoke / deny-floor matrix remained ungated; `#1552` later superseded that local runtime surface), `#1480`, `#1482`, plus upstream `agent-harness#56` (`docker exec` is not unwrapped by the global floor at any tier).
- **Still open — producer/runtime-gated:** `#1457` is ready-for-review but cannot be salvaged in place: its v1.6.5 dispatcher pin is obsolete, five current Codex threads are unresolved, and no reviewed producer floor is both canonical and deployed. Rebuild a replacement from final `main` only after the agent-harness producer/deployment gate converges; then require exact-head automation/reviews followed by a fresh interactive `/hooks` trust session and handler-attributed allow/deny canaries.

Overnight wave (2026-07-23/24, **6 PRs merged** + 3 dependabot PRs — the four feature/substrate PRs below plus `#1447`, the docs sync that recorded the maintainer-merged `#1414` (its content is the 2026-07-18 post-merge comment further down), and `#1448` below; per-PR gate: two independent adversarial review lenses, all-severity findings fixed with posted evidence, full backend suite on the exact head where code changed, required CI green, bot window honored with a content sweep). The wave's own delivery sweep was `#1454`; `#1448`, `#1427`, and dependabot `#1441` merged *after* that sweep ran, so their entries below were added on 2026-07-25:
- **`#1439` (`#1428`, `#1424`) — the approved revision is pinned at approve time and Apply executes exactly it.** Approve reads the latest saved revision, validates it through the approve-time gates, and persists its id as `AutomationProposal.ApprovedRevisionId` (null pin = approved the original operations; a post-approval raced revision can no longer change what Apply executes — closes the `#1428` race). The migration backfills Approved proposals to their latest revision saved **at or before `DecidedAt`** (an explicit, reviewed reversal of latest-revision parity: the invariant is "Apply executes exactly what the approver saw", so a raced post-decision revision is never backfilled; empirically-proven SQLite text-format comparison). Single-proposal read/decide paths return the EFFECTIVE operation set with `Presentation` rebuilt from it (`#1424`); Rejected proposals freeze to the latest revision saved at or before decision time; the MCP terminal stored preview is suppressed whenever an effective revision applies (one payload can no longer carry revision operations beside an original-operations `DiffPreview`). Full suite on head: 7430/0/1. **`#1444` closed this residual:** the review-queue **list** endpoint now returns the same EFFECTIVE operations and rebuilt `Presentation` the single reads do, so a queue card can no longer disagree with the detail view, the diff and Apply. The former "list maps originals" boundary existed only to avoid a per-proposal revision query; a two-phase batched read (payload-free metadata for the page, then payloads for the winning revisions only) removes that cost without loading revision payloads the reader will not use, and both read shapes now select through one shared dispatcher. **`#1462` closed the frontend half** (PR `#1469`, merged 2026-07-25): the `Proposal` type in `types/automation.ts` now exposes `approvedRevisionId` (contract only — no UI claim, per `#1298`), declared **required rather than optional** because `MapToDto` always assigns it and the API serializes with default options (bare `AddControllers()`, no `DefaultIgnoreCondition`), so an unpinned proposal arrives as an explicit `null` and never an absent key — which also turns a field-by-field rebuild that drops the pin into a compile error. It is held against a dead-code sweep by an exported type alias because `tsconfig.app.json` excludes `src/tests/**` from typecheck. Remaining tracked residuals: `#1453` (reject-time pin: the `RevisedAt <= DecidedAt` heuristic is not commit-visibility; display-only), `#1464` (the review surfaces' "recorded operations show the original submission" caveat is inverted now that list reads are effective-set-aware; terminal proposals only — now unblocked by `#1462`), `#1465` (dismissing a rejected proposal drops its decision-time frozen revision back to the originals), `#1467` (the list resolution is bounded in bytes but not in row count, and reads proposal state and revision rows from two snapshots; display-only), `#1468` (frontend specs are type-checked through the dedicated project; the **historical 2026-08-07 baseline was 415 pre-existing errors**, and PR `#1634` leaves **40 files / 391 errors** in the active quarantine). **`#1470` is closed (2026-08-12):** `docs/architecture/DATA_MODEL.md` was recertified against `TaskdeckDbContext` and `TaskdeckDbContextModelSnapshot.cs` end to end. `ApprovedRevisionId` and `DeferredUntil` are documented on `AutomationProposal`, and the stale as-delivered line/entity figure was deleted from all three delivery-ledger mirrors (this file, `docs/AUDIT.md`, `docs/IMPLEMENTATION_MASTERPLAN.md`) rather than refreshed — a hand-maintained count in a ledger rots silently, so the document now carries a *self-checkable coverage invariant* (every `DbSet` has a `###` block; the only non-`DbSet` blocks are the named domain-only entities) with the three-way `rg`/`comm` recipe that proves it, in place of a number.
- **`#1449` (`#1445`) — the nightly k6 Performance Regression Gate is recalibrated to same-code runner variance.** The gate had gone red 3 of 5 nights on an identical main SHA (board-write p95 observed 2.0–3.0 s either side of the old 2200 ms threshold; 2 of the 5 nights passed, and even a passing night cleared the gate by only ~10%). Tail gates moved to global p99 < 5000 ms / board-write p95 < 4500 ms (~1.5–1.7× worst observed-good; calibration evidence inline in the script comment), the 2000 ms measured-capacity warning retained, warning-band edges now pinned by tests (7/7), and every doc/contract mirror synced. The write tail itself (median ~12 ms, p90 1–2 s at 20 VUs — SQLite write convoy) is tracked as `#1446`, including the sub-order-of-magnitude masking window this calibration accepts. **Confirmed on the first post-recalibration night:** the 2026-07-24 06:06Z nightly (run `30071303816`, on `36d563d7`) passed the Performance Regression Gate, against 3 reds in the 5 nights before it. One passing night is not yet a trend — a fresh red is now real signal and should be read from the k6 numbers before any threshold is touched again.
- **`#1451` (`#1438`) — `AuditLogRepositoryIntegrationTests` moved onto the workerless factory** (`#1391` family): the live `AuditRetentionWorker` startup pass can no longer race the ordering test's backdated seeds in full-suite runs; 6× consecutive class runs green. Seeded `#1452`: no test anywhere exercises the retention worker's real `ExecuteAsync` startup path (pre-existing gap a flake had been masquerading as).
- **`#1427` (`#1313`) — the LLM quota-reservation lifecycle is hardened, and a client can no longer erase its own billed usage.** The security half (M1): the streaming and non-streaming `ChatService` surfaces plus the extractor now finalize with `CancellationToken.None` and settle in `finally`, so a client that aborts mid-stream after tokens are billed — including the read-one-token-and-disconnect variant — can no longer discard its own usage record (previously client-controllable). Alongside it: a TTL-swept reservation's real billed tokens are recovered into a committed row instead of being silently dropped (F1, idempotent, warns); `ReservationEstimatedTokens` is floored at 1 so a reservation can never be invisible to the token/global budget sums (F2); the capture-triage reservation settles in `finally` (F4); the release paths are pinned by tests (M2) — provider-throw, zero-token, no-LLM bootstrap, board-context-build throw, and a provider yielding only an error event all **release** the reservation, while cancel-after-billed-tokens is pinned to the **opposite** invariant (`StreamResponseAsync_CancelledAfterBilledTokens_ShouldCommitNotRelease`): once tokens are billed, cancellation must commit and must never release, because releasing there is precisely the bypass M1 closes; and reservation cleanup now wraps request/board-context construction, so a setup failure releases instead of leaking until TTL (Codex P2). **The check-then-insert atomicity guarantee is explicitly NOT closed here** — it was proven not closeable in-process (the reservation over-admits even under a global full-span lock, because of cold-start WAL `-shm` read-visibility), so the redesign it needs (eager `-shm` warm-up, or non-snapshot enforcement) is deferred to `#1435` and routed to the maintainer; the concurrency-guarantee tests are skipped pending that issue, which is why this hardening ships green. Merged on the maintainer's explicit merge-with-tracked ruling; residual review P2s tracked as `#1431`.
- **`#1448` — FRUGALITY MODE is now *in* the Fable overnight manual** (`docs/agentic/OVERNIGHT_LOOP.fable.md`) instead of being re-pasted into each launch prompt, per the enforcement-ladder rule that an amendment surviving two runs belongs in the manual. Its review round also produced a reusable structural lesson: Codex found sampling-exemption classes one at a time across three rounds, and a single allowlist inversion — only pure mechanical-motion packets are sampleable, while suite/CI verdicts and reviewer/settlement packets never are — closed the whole class at once.
- **Dependabot:** `#1443` (.NET 8.0.28 → 8.0.29 patch train + Test.Sdk 18.8.1) and `#1440` (npm minor-patch group incl. vue 3.5.40) merged green. `#1441` (actions group) was staged green and then **merged on explicit maintainer authorization** under the policy in force for that historical wave. ADR-0051 has since superseded that category gate: current workflow PRs use advisory `CODEOWNERS` routing plus the exact-head technical/review gate, without a designated-human-review requirement. `#1442` (pinia 3→4 MAJOR) was genuinely red and is **closed to snooze**; the migration slice it actually needs is tracked as `#1455` rather than rubber-stamped.
- **Review-bot estate note:** the consumer Gemini Code Assist GitHub integration is sunset (its farewell notice now posts on every PR) and Copilot review is quota-exhausted — Codex is currently the only live external review bot; the in-house two-lens adversarial pass carries correspondingly more weight.

<!-- 2026-07-18: post-merge sync (maintainer-merged after the frugal wave) — #1414 (closes #1397, frontend-only per the maintainer decision recorded on #1397): expired and terminal proposals stay inspectable but no longer fire a live diff that 400s — both review shells (Legacy useReviewActions/ReviewProposalCard and Paper PaperReviewView.onPreviewDiff) short-circuit read-only proposals to the stored diffPreview under an explicit read-only banner (or a "no stored preview available" note), the expired-path button is relabeled View/Hide stored preview, and a 400 from /diff renders an explicit invalid state (a pending zero-op proposal is flagged as containing no operations to apply — Apply will reject it — instead of an approvable "No changes" surface). Server-authoritative expiry is honored and stored-preview reveals are re-authorized on the #1414 P2 round. New shared helpers: isProposalReadOnly (useReviewProposals) + isValidationError (useErrorMapper). The #1395 backend 400 contract is unchanged. Residual review P2s tracked as #1436 (merged-with-tracked per the maintainer's call). -->

Frugal wave (2026-07-18, wave #2 — the follow-on to the approve == apply trust-class closure wave below; **2 PRs merged** — per-PR gate: independent adversarial review, all-severity findings fixed, required CI green):
- **`#1430` (split from `#1379`) — artefact extraction is now bounded by a permit/concurrency gate.** A new `ArtefactExtractionGate` (Application singleton) takes a non-blocking permit before the parse worker spawns and releases it on both the completed and abandoned paths (a runaway parse holds its permit for its full lifetime); saturation ⇒ a pre-parse `TooManyRequests` with no history row and no new thread. The bound is `ExtractionMaxConcurrency` (`int`, default 2, `[Range(1, 64)]`, startup-validated). This is the verified-sound permit half split out of the parked `#1417` extraction-resource-ceiling work; the decompression-bomb containment half is deliberately **not** here — PdfPig 0.1.15 decodes xref streams with a hard-coded provider before any injected ceiling exists, so a provider-injection ceiling is bypassed (proven on `#1417`) — and continues under `#1429`. Adds **ADR-0047** (artefact-extraction resource bounding: permit gate + provider-injection ceiling as ObjStm-covering defense-in-depth with the documented xref gap) and **ADR-0048** (decompression-bomb containment boundary: a memory-capped extraction worker process — Job Object on Windows / cgroup under Docker — reversing the earlier sidecar rejection on the new evidence).
- **`#1432` (`#1426`) — `ValidatePermissionsAsync` always gates board access, even for an empty operation list.** The engine previously short-circuited an empty operation list to `Result.Success()` with the board half of the access gate skipped (`boardId` forced to `null`), silently treating an operation-less proposal as permitted and forcing every new consumer to bolt on its own board-access fallback (the `#1415`/`#1425` trap). Now the full requester/board access gate always runs; only the per-operation contract validator is skipped when the list is empty. Emptiness itself is still **not** rejected here by design (a legitimate transient shape for revise-into-validity, `#1423`) — the "at least one operation" rejection stays with the structure gate, which runs before permissions in every approve/apply/diff/executor chain, so **approve == apply is unchanged** and the change is strictly additive restriction (a previously-`Success` empty-op result can only become a denial). Chosen over forbidding zero-op proposals at create, which would break the create-empty-then-revise flow `#1423` pins.
- **Nightly k6 confirmed GREEN:** the first nightly run after the `#1359` repair (run `29632619187` on `326b6b5b`) passed, and the `#1359` failure-ledger row was flipped to resolved (commit `4fdae133`).
- **Seeded this wave:** `#1429` (memory-capped extraction worker process — the ADR-0048 containment boundary), `#1431`, `#1433`, `#1434`, `#1435`, `#1436`. `#1427` (LLM quota-reservation robustness hardening, `#1313`) sat in the final review gate at the close of this wave and **has since shipped** — see the 2026-07-23/24 wave above; its atomicity redesign remains open as `#1435`.
- **Standing human-gated holds:** `#1337` (licensing pack — blocked on human-only trademark/name clearance) and `#1295` (deny-floor overlay). On 2026-07-25 the maintainer dispositioned `#1295` explicitly: **keep the evidence, return the PR to draft, retain the human gate, and do not revive it in place.** The branch is conflicting and hundreds of commits behind `main`, all 16 review threads remain unresolved (11 still current, including command-wrapper, root-delete, SQL/container, npm-executor, and Windows parsing bypasses), and its delegation model targets a global floor version the installed floor has long since moved past — so its old green checks establish nothing about current behavior. If `#1293` proceeds, the smallest Taskdeck-specific overlay is to be rebuilt from current `main` once the chosen agent-harness floor is itself reviewed and stable; conflict churn on the ancient branch is not the path.

Approve == apply trust-class closure wave (2026-07-18, **6 PRs merged** — per-PR gate: independent adversarial review, all-severity findings fixed, required CI green): closes the "a user approves or previews something the executor will then refuse" (preview == apply) trust-violation class on the server, across the diff, approve, and MCP surfaces (the `#1370`→`#1374`, `#1376`→`#1395`, `#1398` chain).
- **`#1413` (`#1398`) — the proposal diff now runs Apply's permission/existence gates.** `GetProposalDiffAsync` (both the revised-payload and original-operations paths, before the cached-`DiffPreview` fast-path return) now calls the same `ValidatePermissionsAsync` Apply calls: requester-exists → `404`, board-exists → `404`, board-access → `403`, then the operation-contract validator. Closes the third and last asymmetry from the `#1395` parity round — a reviewer whose board access was revoked (or whose board/requester was deleted) mid-review previewed a clean `200` then failed at Apply. Status-agnostic on purpose: terminal proposals no longer disclose stored board-derived content on preview — access errors (`403`/`404`) surface once the earlier structure/expiry gates pass (a preview denial, stricter than Apply's idempotent short-circuit); an expired, access-revoked proposal still reports expiry first (pinned by `GetProposalDiffAsync_ShouldReportExpiry_NotForbidden_WhenExpiredAndAccessRevoked`).
- **`#1423` (`#1416`) — Approve runs Apply's structure + permission/contract gates before committing the transition.** `ApproveProposalAsync` validates the effective (revision-aware) operation set: structure → `400`, expiry → `409` (the existing domain-transition contract, deliberately not the diff path's `400` shape), permissions/contract → `400`/`403`/`404`. A zero-op or access-revoked proposal that used to approve `200` and only fail at Apply now fails at approve. Gate ordering (structure → expiry → permissions) mirrors diff/apply; only `PendingReview` runs the gates. Seeded `#1424` (approve/get DTO returns original ops even when a revision exists).
- **`#1425` (`#1415`) — the MCP `proposal_detail` preview is gated through the diff path.** It no longer emits the raw stored `DiffPreview`: open proposals route through the fully-gated `GetProposalDiffAsync`; decided/terminal proposals serve the stored preview but re-check requester/board access via a new `GetTerminalProposalStoredPreviewAsync` seam, so a reviewer who lost board access is denied the stored preview instead of reading stale board contents through it. The payload gains a `diffPreviewSource` provenance marker (`"live"` vs `"stored"`).
- **`#1421` (`#1403`) — SQLite timestamp bounds are parameterized so zero-fraction boundary rows compare correctly.** EF trims trailing fraction zeros (a whole-second instant stores as `...00:00:00+00:00`, no `.`), so the old fixed-width `.fffffff` bound strings mis-sorted at the exact boundary tick. `AuditLogRepository` (`CountByDateAsync`/`DeleteOldEntriesAsync`/`BuildSqliteQueryAsync`) and `OAuthAuthCodeRepository` (`DeleteExpiredAsync`/`TryConsumeAtomicAsync`) now pass `DateTimeOffset` predicates, UTC-normalized (`.ToUniversalTime()`) so the offset-preserving mapping can't mis-compare non-UTC bounds. Seeded `#1422` (repo-wide sweep of remaining timestamp seams).
- **`#1420` (`#1409`) — removed the dead `ApiKeyService.ValidateKeyAsync` usage-recording path** and its `ApiKey.RecordUsage()` domain helper (zero production callers; a dual-writer trap — MCP auth records usage via `ApiKeyMiddleware`'s `ExecuteUpdateAsync`). `LastUsedAt` is untouched.
- **`#1419` (`#1418`) — test isolation:** `WorkspaceApiTests` moved onto `HostedWorkerDisabledTestWebApplicationFactory` so the live capture-lane worker can't claim a seeded `Processing` row mid-test (the `#1335` boundary). Test-only.

Overnight correctness + test-reliability wave #2 (2026-07-17, **7 PRs merged** — per-PR gate: independent adversarial review, all-severity findings fixed, required CI green; the late-evening/overnight follow-on to the 11-PR sweep below):
- **`#1394` (`#1335`) — hosted-worker test-isolation factory.** A new `HostedWorkerDisabledTestWebApplicationFactory` strips app-assembly hosted services from the test host via a shared predicate (also asserted by a dedicated guard test); adopted by `LlmQueueRepositoryIntegrationTests`, `OutboundWebhookDeliveryRepositoryTests`, `AutomationProposalRepositoryIntegrationTests`, and a new `ProcessNextClaimRaceTests` (split out from `QueueClaimRaceTests`, whose CaptureTriage cases need live workers). Kills the hosted-worker pre-emption flake family in full-suite runs.
- **`#1391` (`#1383`) — AuditRetention integration determinism.** `AuditRetentionRepositoryIntegrationTests` adopts the workerless factory and is hardened (`BackdateEntryAsync` asserts affected==1, InvariantCulture, `ReloadAsync` read-back) — root cause was the live `AuditRetentionWorker` startup pass (90-day cutoff) racing 100/200-day backdated seeds. Empirically pinned the EF SQLite `DateTimeOffset` storage format: `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss.FFFFFFFzzz` (trailing zeros trimmed).
- **`#1392` (`#1332`) — deterministic Redis dispose-vs-connect seam.** `RedisCacheServiceTests` now reaches the connect seam through a dedicated named background `Thread` instead of `Task.Run` (eliminates the thread-pool-saturation flake) and disposes its `ManualResetEventSlim`s. Test-only.
- **`#1395` (`#1376`) — proposal diff now runs apply's structure and expiry gates on every path.** `AutomationProposalService.GetProposalDiffAsync` runs the same gates apply runs (structure, then expiry) on all diff paths including the cached-`DiffPreview` fast path — expired → `400` "Proposal has expired", zero-op → `400` "Proposal must contain at least one operation" (was `200`/`404`). Extends the `#1374` preview==apply parity contract for the structure/expiry/cached-preview paths; full parity still excludes apply's `ValidatePermissionsAsync` access/existence gates (tracked below). Seeded `#1397` (frontend expired/zero-op preview UX + coverage) and `#1398` (fold `ValidatePermissionsAsync` access/existence gates into diff — the third asymmetry).
- **`#1396` (`#1387`) — buffered account-export extraction-history N+1 removed.** A new `IArtefactExtractionRepository.GetByArtefactsForUserAsync` batch method (user-scoped IN query, 900-id cap, SQLite TEXT-ordering parity with the old paged path) is fed by the export's 500-id chunks. Seeded `#1399` (the streaming-path N+1, which needs a streaming-safe design).
- **`#1400` (`#1393`) — production timestamp culture-invariance.** InvariantCulture on 5 hand-built SQLite timestamp string sites (`AuditLogRepository` CountByDateAsync/DeleteOldEntriesAsync + `DateOnly.Parse`; `OAuthAuthCodeRepository` TryConsumeAtomicAsync/DeleteExpiredAsync, now also `.UtcDateTime`-normalized; `KnowledgeFtsSearchService` CreatedAt parse) with hostile-culture regression tests. Seeded `#1403` (the fixed-digit vs EF-trimmed boundary residual).
- **`#1401` (`#1384`) — MCP per-key rate budget charged once, at the right point.** The 60/min per-key budget is now spent inside `ApiKeyMiddleware` immediately after key resolution, BEFORE the Users lookup + `UpdateLastUsedAsync`, so valid over-quota keys no longer drive auth-stage DB work; the endpoint-stage policy is removed (one budget, checked once) across both co-hosted and standalone pipelines; the `#1381` pre-auth-IP-budget semantics and the `429` contract are preserved. Seeded `#1402` (`UpdateLastUsedAsync` silently never persists — a swallowed EF translation failure) and `#1404` (stale-owner-key IP-budget escape).
- **Seeded this wave:** `#1397`, `#1398`, `#1399`, `#1402`, `#1403`, `#1404`. **Still open — NOT shipped:** `#1386` (wave-1 final review round) plus the standing human-gated holds `#1295` (deny-floor overlay), `#1337` (licensing pack), and `#1359`.

Overnight substrate + correctness wave (2026-07-17, **11 PRs merged** — per-PR gate: independent adversarial review, all-severity findings fixed, required CI green; full backend suite reported 7223 passed / 0 failed / 1 skipped post-`#1373`):
- **`#1373` (`#1282`) — the API test factory now mirrors production SQLite settings.** A new shared `UseTaskdeckSqlite` registration helper in Infrastructure is used by BOTH production DI and the integration test factory, so WAL/busy-timeout drift between them is now structurally impossible; it retired the proven registration-parity cause, not every possible Windows concurrent-write HTTP-500 cause. The post-`#1373` same-symptom recurrence remains open as `#1512`.
- **`#1375` (`#1298`, REVIVAL-02 Phase-1 ship gate) — removed the fake Paper review undo timeline** and replaced the reversibility copy with factual apply-risk guidance ("Apply considerations"), while the confidence label now renders "Operation safety"; the stable side-effect wire shapes are preserved for GP-03. Full contract detail is in the 2026-07-17 REVIVAL-02 note directly above — not duplicated here.
- **`#1374` (`#1370`; seeded `#1376`) — proposal preview aligned with apply for reorder/collision/structure.** The clamped reorder position is surfaced in the diff, create-card id collisions fail at validation with a stable `409`, and a shared `ProposalOperationStructureValidator` runs before original-proposal diffs (one source of truth with Apply). A true preview==apply parity contract test was added.
- **`#1372` (`#1367`) — the standalone MCP host-security guard now mirrors `HostFilteringMiddleware` exactly.** It fails closed on all zero-host `AllowedHosts` shapes (`";"`, `";;"`, blank) while preserving port-suffixed / whitespace-bearing literals; a new in-process standalone-host integration test carries a mutation proof.
- **`#1377` (`#1357`) — the concurrent-migrator test asserts final schema state** (any-`SqliteException` collision signature, at most one fault, exact `__EFMigrationsHistory` equivalence) instead of no-throw; adds a deterministic fail-open collision test and an `AcquireLock` exclusivity contract test (internal seam). Retires the windows-latest migrator flake.
- **`#1378` (`#1369`; `#1379` expanded to a hard wiring gate) — artefact extraction is bounded by a wall-clock budget** (`Artefacts:ExtractionTimeoutSeconds`, default 30s) with warning-bearing `extraction-timeout` history rows and abandoned-task handling; real PdfPig / plain-text extractors are exercised through the budgeted path.
- **`#1362` (`#1274`, P-I ship gate; seeded `#1363` earlier, `#1380`, `#1382`) — core E2E is re-pointed at Paper-default sessions** with explicit frozen-Legacy opt-outs (builds on the 2026-07-14 Paper-default alignment paragraph above, which it does not restate). Adds full axe WCAG 2.1 A/AA on Home/Today/Inbox/Review/seeded Board/Login, a real capture → review → apply lane with a hard-asserted confirmation dialog, a `PaperBoardCard` nested-interactive fix + focus-follows-selection, `--faint` token contrast floors, and a Boards shortcut ledger.
- **`#1366` (`#1365`, `#1371`) — the presence stress test proves join/leave broadcast delivery** (not just tracker membership) with fail-closed controls; 15/15 consecutive runs post-`#1373` parity.
- **`#1385` (`#1355`) — buffered account-export artefact blob loads are now batched.** A new `SourceArtefactRepository` batch blob-load feeds the buffered GDPR export in bounded chunks (idiomatic `Chunk()`), with a batch-id-count guard against the SQLite parameter limit (pass-side boundary pinned at 900 ids) and export-JSON round-trip + missing-blob contract tests. Closes the LOW efficiency finding seeded from the `#1341` security review.
- **`#1381` (`#1368`) — the MCP pre-auth per-IP limiter is now a failure budget with admission control.** Budget is spent only on authentication failures (successful auth never consumes, so multiple keys behind one egress address keep independent per-key budgets); an exhausted address is rejected before the API-key lookup via non-consuming inspection; a per-IP concurrency gate (`RateLimiting:McpAuthenticationPerIpConcurrency`, default 16) caps in-flight pre-auth work; consumption is abort-proof (an auth-failed marker set before the 401 write is consumed in a `finally`, so abort-storms still spend budget); trusted forwarded headers now also apply to the standalone MCP host (default OFF — XFF is never trusted from an unknown peer).
- **`#1390` — same-night hotfix of the `#1377` fail-open collision test.** The barrier design flaked an unrelated PR in the opposite direction of the flake it covered (run 29552875836: fast runners let the winner finish the whole chain before the loser applied anything, failing the required-collision assertion). The test now stages the interception structurally — migrator A runs alone until `__EFMigrationsHistory` polling proves it mid-chain, then B is released — and a pathological staging miss passes gracefully with a loud `WARNING: STAGING MISSED` diagnostic, so the test can never flake an unrelated PR again.
- **Seeded follow-ups:** `#1376`, `#1379`, `#1380`, `#1382`, `#1383` (AuditRetention flake), `#1384`, `#1387`, `#1388`. **Still open — NOT shipped:** `#1386` (final review round in flight), plus the standing human-gated holds `#1295` (deny-floor overlay), `#1337` (licensing pack), and `#1359`.

Revival + generalist-expansion wave delivery (2026-07-13, **15 PRs merged** — the revival/GEN direction is now live on `main`; per-PR review gate: independent adversarial reviews, all-severity findings fixed, real merge conflicts resolved, required CI green after the `#1352` CI-Extended fix):
- **Direction ratified:** `#1296` (ADR-0044 revival pivot — open-beta direction + REVIVAL_PLAN spine + canonical doc sync), `#1328` (ADR-0046 generalist expansion — artefact intake + dossiers, Phase 4; the twin generalist app stays deferred behind the GEN-12 `#1326` evidence gate (gate closed 2026-09-03: stay one application, ADR-0046 stands)), `#1312` (ADR-0045 LLM transcript-triage engine, REVIVAL-08 M1). **ADR-0044 / ADR-0045 / ADR-0046 are all now Accepted.**
- **Generalist expansion (GEN wave, ADR-0046):** `#1341` GEN-01 artefact-storage foundation (immutable user-owned `SourceArtefact` + blobs; claims-scoped, IDOR-safe upload/download/delete with a race-safe in-transaction board-access recheck; streaming size caps preserving HTTP 413; magic-byte content validation; FK cascade + GDPR export/delete — independently security-reviewed, no CRITICAL/HIGH) → `#1346` GEN-02 bounded local text extraction (plain-text / Markdown / PdfPig, immutable warning-bearing extraction history); `#1339` GEN-05 apply due dates + labels from proposals (closes the extract-but-unappliable gap — also fixed a latent bug where a tautological guard silently cleared a column's WIP limit on *every* reorder, and made proposal reorder atomic/lossless with the destination position shown in the approval diff); `#1340` GEN-09 untrusted-artefact threat model + injection canaries; `#1342` GEN-10 generalist-friendly guided navigation.
- **Revival (ADR-0044):** `#1334` REVIVAL-01 registration gating by deployment mode (Open / InviteOnly / Closed; operator-minted bootstrap invite; new `AddRegistrationGating` migration) → `#1344` REVIVAL-05 Paper first-run onboarding (real capture → reviewed proposal → board-apply milestone; fail-closed registration availability; theme-aware on-ember CTA contrast + restored Legacy focus ring); `#1329` REVIVAL-04 self-hosted Paper fonts + branding metadata; `#1331` README rewrite for the revival beta; `#1333` de-stubbed the fabricated Today dossier (`#1272`, latest-request-wins guard).
- **Infra / harness / ops:** the maintainer first merged the linchpin `#1352` (systemic CI-Extended reusable-workflow permission `startup_failure`, `#1330`) plus dependabot `#1351` / `#1349` to unblock the deck; this session then merged `#1294` (harness T3 region maps + skill read-first diet, `#1291`) and `#1336` (overnight human-action + failure-ledger record, `#1327`).
- **MCP HTTP security boundary (`#1338`):** standalone and co-hosted Streamable HTTP now share the only `MapMcp("/mcp")` registration; API-key auth, telemetry, user-scoped resources, pre-authentication IP throttling, and opaque-key-ID rate limiting run on that exact route, while `/` is no longer an alternate MCP endpoint. The route explicitly disables browser CORS. Standalone defaults to loopback and replaces blank or any-host configuration (`*`, `0.0.0.0`, `[::]`, including mixed lists) with an exact loopback allowlist; remote binds require explicit host configuration and TLS termination.
- **Seeded follow-up:** `#1355` (batch the buffered-export artefact blob loads — LOW efficiency finding from the `#1341` security review). **Human-gated, NOT merged:** `#1295` (deny-floor overlay — 16 unresolved review threads including a CRITICAL, needs remediation) and `#1337` (licensing pack — blocked on human trademark clearance).
